Administrative

,

en-US

action
 
messages
 
are
 
used
 
to
 
inform
 
market
 
participants
 
when
 
a
 
security
 
is
 
halted/paused
 
or
 
released
 
for 
trading.

messages

basic
 
security
 
data
 
such
 
as
 
the
 
market
 
tier
 
and
 
Financial
 
Status
 
Indicator.

participant
 
position
 
message
 
carries
 
the
 
Primary
 
Market
 
Maker
 
status,
 
Market
 
Participant
 
status
 
and
 
en-US
Market Maker mode fields used by some firms to comply with market regulations.

such

the

The 
messages that 
make up the 
TotalView
 
ITCH protocol 
are 
typically delivered using 
a 
higher 
level protocol that takes 
care of 
sequencing 
and 
delivery 
guarantees.
 
en-US
Nasdaq offers the TotalView
 
ITCH data feed in three protocol options:.
 
en-US
 
en-US

en-US
 
SoupBinTCP
en-US
 
en-US

en-US
 
Compressed
en-US
 
via
 
SoupBinTCP
 
en-US

en-US
 
MoldUDP64
en-US
 
en-US
In
 
the
 
market
 
data
 
messages,
 
instruments
 
are
 
identified
 
by
 
a
 
stock
 
locate
 
code
 

 
a
 
low
 
lying
 
integer
 
employed
 
with 
the
 
intent
 
of
 
serving
 
as an
 
array
 
index
 
for
 
rapidly
 
looking
 
up
 
instrument
 
details.
 
The
 
locate
 
codes
 
are 
dynamically 
assigned
 
each
 
day,
 
starting
 
with
 
a
 
value
 
of
 
1,
 
and
 
communicated
 
via
 
the
 
Stock
 
Directory
 
message.
 
An
 

locate
 
code
 
will
 
not
 
change
 
intraday;
 
however,
 
there
 
should
 
be
 
no
 
expectation
 
that
 
the
 
assignment
 
will
 
be
 
the
 
same
 
en-US
across
 
multiple
 
days.
 
The
 
Stock
 
Locate
 
code
 
appears
 
all
 
messages,
 
and
 
at
 
the
 
same
 
position
 
in
 
all
 
messages
 
to 
support
 
efficient
 
filtering.
 
A
 
default
 
value
 
of
 
0
 
will
 
be
 
applied
 
to
 
messages
 
which
 
are
 
not
 
stock
 
dependent.

All alpha fields are ASCII fields which are left justified and padded on the right with spaces.
 
en-US

are
 
integer
 
fields,
 
supplied
 
with
 
an
 
associated
 
precision.

converted

a

format,

are
 
in

point
 
format,
 
where
 
the
 
precision
 
defines
 
the
 
number
 
of
 
decimal
 
places.

a
 
field
 
flagged
 
as
 
Price

1.4
 
Modify Order
 
Messages
 
en-US
Modify Order messages always include 
the 
Order Reference 
Number of the Add Order to 
which 
the 
update 
applies.
 
To
 
determine
 
the
 
current
 
display
 
shares
 
for
 
an
 
order,
 
ITCH
 
subscribers
 
must
 
deduct
 
the
 
number
 
of
 
shares 
stated
 
in
 
the
 
Modify
 
message
 
from
 
the
 
original
 
number
 
of
 
shares
 
stated
 
in
 
the
 
Add
 
Order
 
message
 
with
 
the
 
same 
reference
 
number.
 
Nasdaq
 
may
 
send
 
multiple
 
Modify
 
Order
 
messages
 
for
 
the
 
same
 
order
 
reference
 
number
 
and 
the
 
effects
 
are
 
cumulative.
 
When
 
the
 
number
 
of
 
display
 
shares
 
for
 
an
 
order
 
reaches
 
zero,
 
the
 
order
 
is
 
dead
 
and 
should
 
be removed
 
from
 
the
 
book.

is

reinstated.

trade message. If a firm is only using the ITCH feed to build a book, however, it may ignore 
these messages as they have no impact on the current book.

en-US
 
Between 9:25 and 9:28 a.m. and 3:50 and 3:55 p.m., Nasdaq disseminates the NOII information every 10 
seconds.
 
en-US

en-US
 
Between 9:28 and 9:30 a.m. and 3:55 and 4:00 p.m., Nasdaq disseminates the NOII information every 
second.
 
en-US

en-US
 
For Nasdaq Halt, IPO and Pauses, NOII messages will be disseminated at 1 second intervals starting 1 
second after quoting period starts/trading action is released.
 
en-US

en-US
 
For more information, please see the 
en-US
FAQ on Opening and Closing Crosses
en-US
.
 
en-US

en-US
 
Nasdaq will also disseminate an Extended Trading Close \(ETC\) message from 4:00 p.m. to 4:05 p.m. at five 
second intervals.
 
en-US

en-US
 
For more information, please see the 
en-US
FAQ on Extended Trading Close
en-US
.

ITCH Version 2.0
 
en-US

en-US
 
Changed
 
price
 
field
 
format
 
to
 
6
 
whole
 
number
 
digits
 
followed
 
immediately
 
by
 
4
 
decimal
 
digits.
 
The
 
decimal 
point
 
was
 
no
 
longer
 
included
 
in
 
the
 
field
 
but
 
implied
 
by
 
position.
 
This
 
reduced
 
the
 
bandwidth
 
of
 
the
 
stream 
significantly
 
and
 
makes
 
for
 
much
 
easier
 
parsing
 
and
 
processing
 
of
 
price
 
values.

timestamps
 
to
 
be
 
stated
 
as
 
milliseconds
 
past
 
midnight
 
rather
 
than
 
hundredths
 
of
 
seconds
 
past 
midnight.
 
en-US

en-US
 
Replaced
 
the
 
End
 
of
 
Session
 
System
 
event
 
with
 
the
 
End
 
of
 
Day
 
System
 
Event.
 
Although
 
the
 
messages
 
looked 
identical,
 
the
 
semantics
 
were
 
different.
 
The
 
old
 
End
 
of
 
Session
 
System
 
Event
 
told
 
subscribers
 
that
 
there
 
would 
be
 
no
 
more
 
messages
 
during
 
that
 
session.
 
The
 
new
 
End
 
of
 
Day
 
System
 
Event
 
indicated
 
that
 
there
 
are
 
no
 
more 
orders today, 
but 
there 
may 
still 
be more 
messages like Cancels 
on open orders and 
Breaks 
on 
previously 
executed orders
.

changes.

also attempts 
to 
provide 
the 
reason 
for 
each trading action update.
